# Source parity ledger

<!-- GOAL12_LEDGER baseline=53fa77ccd8a279eb87e92294ef3687b03ff80112 source=670/507/163/0 examples=99/99/0 -->

## 0. Known defect: 108 stale cadr-side mappings (advisor, 2026-08-13)

**This ledger currently misstates where 10.4% of its cadr-side pointers live.**
108 of the 1,038 `file:line` mappings in the tables below point at files that
were deleted on 2026-08-03 when the CLI monoliths were split:

```
38  src/cadr-cli/interactive-services.lisp   (deleted in b63bc80b1)
26  src/cadr-cli/core-support.lisp           (7939b2b0d)
13  src/cadr-cli/model-services.lisp         (7939b2b0d)
10  src/cadr-cli/utilities.lisp              (7939b2b0d)
 9  src/cadr-cli/sdk-services.lisp           (7939b2b0d)
 8  src/cadr-cli/settings.lisp               (7939b2b0d)
 4  src/cadr-cli/package-manager.lisp        (7939b2b0d)
```

Recompute at any time:

```sh
python3 - <<'EOF'
import re,os,collections
rows=[l for l in open('docs/source-parity.md') if l.startswith('| `packages/')]
pat=re.compile(r'((?:src|test|examples|scripts)/[a-zA-Z0-9/_.-]+\.(?:lisp|sexp|asd|py|sh)):\d+')
miss=collections.Counter(p for l in rows for p in pat.findall(l) if not os.path.exists(p))
tot=sum(len(pat.findall(l)) for l in rows)
print(f'{sum(miss.values())}/{tot} stale'); [print(' ',n,p) for p,n in miss.most_common()]
EOF
```

**Root cause**: `scripts/check-source-parity-drift.sh` compares only the pi-side
`packages/**.ts` inventory; it never checks whether the cadr-side path in a row
still exists.  These 108 rows therefore rotted for ten days across the goal
15/16/17/18/19 closeouts without any gate noticing.

**Status**: the missing existence check is scheduled as goal 20 Part F item 3,
which is a precondition for the large-scale rewrite in that goal's Part K.
Until it lands, **any change that touches a cadr-side mapping in this ledger
must verify path existence by hand**.

**Scope note**: this defect predates and is unrelated to goal 19; it is recorded
here rather than in a goal document because it is a standing property of this
ledger, and because closing any goal while silently carrying a known-wrong
ledger would be the failure this project's discipline exists to prevent.

## 1. Scope and counting rules

This is the **source ledger**. It disposes every source file in the fixed
baseline but does not prove runtime behavior, non-functional properties, or
presentation equivalence; those independent axes live in `docs/parity-map.md`,
`docs/performance.md`, and `docs/presentation-parity.md`. The runnable-example
`examples/manifest.sexp` is an auxiliary inventory, not another ledger.

Source baseline: `earendil-works/pi@53fa77ccd8a279eb87e92294ef3687b03ff80112`
(`v0.84.1-38-g53fa77ccd`), read from `/tmp/cadr-parity-53fa77ccd`.
The denominator is every tracked `*.ts` file below `packages/` except paths
ending in `*.test.ts`. This includes TypeScript configs, executable examples,
and non-`.test.ts` helpers under `test/`; it excludes generated/untracked files.
The sorted 670-path manifest has SHA-256
`44d074219e3a808f91653dc0366b562fa1d3629965879e319dfb31ae253912ea`.
Its input is UTF-8 path text with exactly one LF after each path and bytewise
`LC_ALL=C` ordering; it contains no repository prefix, table prose, verdict,
or evidence columns.
The initial Cadr-side audit was made at
`fa6b09eda09a191ede16b7b15729da10a76e7f42`; Part C verdicts below are updated
only after their implementation and regression commits land.

```sh
git -C /tmp/cadr-parity-53fa77ccd ls-tree -r --name-only 53fa77ccd -- packages \
  | awk '$0 !~ /\.test\.ts$/ && $0 ~ /\.ts$/' | LC_ALL=C sort
```

Verdicts use no fourth escape hatch:

- `已实现`: the capability exists, with concrete Lisp symbol/file evidence;
- `有意差异`: a different Lisp/runtime/build structure supplies an equivalent capability;
- `缺失`: a material capability is absent, including reusable structure consumed as a capability.

A partially mapped file is `缺失` when any material capability in its listed surface is
absent. Barrel files are charged only for the public namespace; missing child capabilities
are charged to their defining files. Source correspondence does not prove internal behavior
equivalence, so this ledger complements rather than replaces behavior tests,
non-functional gates, presentation evidence, and user A/B verification.
Shipped runnable examples count as source capabilities; a lower-level extension API does not
make the example itself implemented. The deterministic image fixture generator follows the
same strict rule: embedded fixtures do not replace the baseline regeneration command.

## 1a. Package boundary map (routing upstream changes into cadr)

This section is a snapshot of the current state, not a structural mandate;
when a boundary moves in a Lisp-natural direction, change the code and update
this map. (Advisor-authored 2026-08-08; verify against the code before
relying on it during an upgrade.)

The baseline splits its two main packages at the **library/product** line:
`packages/agent` is the generic agent library, `packages/coding-agent` is the
whole product. Cadr splits the same functionality at the **headless/terminal**
line instead, because `cadr-server` and `cadr-evals` need sessions, compaction,
and extensions without any UI:

| Baseline | Cadr system |
|---|---|
| `packages/agent` (agent loop, tool protocol, messages) | `cadr-agent` |
| `packages/coding-agent/src/core/` session, compaction, extensions, export | `cadr-agent` |
| `packages/coding-agent/src/core/tools/` (concrete bash/edit/…) | `cadr-cli/tools/` |
| `packages/coding-agent` modes, bootstrap, resource loading, settings UI | `cadr-cli` |
| `packages/ai` | `cadr-ai` |
| `packages/tui` | `cadr-tui` |
| `packages/server` | `cadr-server` |
| `packages/storage` | `cadr-storage` (shell) + `cadr-agent` sqlite/session backends |
| `packages/protocol` | `cadr-protocol` |
| `packages/evals` | `cadr-evals` |
| — (originated in cadr; see note) | `cadr-core` (failure-safety primitives, goal 14; since goal 18 Part C also the single owner of the baseline `config.ts` path decisions — `getAgentDir` and the nine residents derive from `src/cadr-core/paths.lisp`) |

One placement is deliberately isomorphic to the baseline: the tool *protocol*
lives in the agent package (`cadr-agent/tool.lisp` ↔ `packages/agent`'s
`AgentTool`) while *concrete* tools live in the product package
(`cadr-cli/tools/` ↔ `coding-agent/src/core/tools/`).

**Routing rule for upstream diffs**: a change under `packages/coding-agent`
goes to `cadr-agent` when it touches core/session/compaction/extensions/export,
and to `cadr-cli` when it touches modes/bootstrap/resources/settings or a
concrete tool. Two refinements verified against all 595 rows (goal 18 Part F):
`packages/agent/src/harness/tools/` holds concrete tool implementations that
route to `cadr-cli/tools/` exactly like `coding-agent/src/core/tools/`, and
`coding-agent/src/config.ts` path decisions route to `cadr-core/paths.lisp`. Per-file ground truth is the `:baseline-source` annotation on
each ledger row below; this rule is the first-pass router, the ledger is the
authority. (The v0.84 upgrade carries 74 changed `coding-agent` files that
will be routed with this rule.)

## 2a. User-facing documentation disposition (goal 18 Part J)

The baseline `packages/coding-agent/docs/` tree is website content for pi, not a
source capability; it has no per-file rows in section 4. cadr's obligation is
narrower and now closed: every documentation path named by product text must
exist and describe cadr's real behavior.

**Created (12 files, all describing cadr behavior, not translated pi docs):**
the eleven documents the system prompt names — `docs/extensions.md`,
`themes.md`, `skills.md`, `prompt-templates.md`, `tui.md`, `keybindings.md`,
`sdk.md`, `custom-provider.md`, `models.md`, `packages.md`,
`environment-variables.md` — plus `docs/providers.md`, which the no-auth and
no-models guidance prints. Existence is gate-enforced by
`test/cadr-cli/goal18-docs-test.lisp` (any product-text `docs/`/`examples/`
reference that does not resolve fails the deterministic FiveAM stage).

**Remaining baseline docs — disposition:**

| Baseline doc | Disposition |
|---|---|
| `quickstart.md`, `usage.md`, `index.md`, `docs.json`, `images/` | pi website/quickstart material; cadr's README covers install and first run. Not ported. |
| `settings.md`, `sessions.md`, `session-format.md`, `json.md`, `rpc.md`, `compaction.md`, `security.md` | Baseline reference docs whose cadr behavior is already covered by README plus the twelve files above (settings keys in `keybindings.md`/`packages.md`, sessions in `tui.md`, wire modes in `sdk.md`). Not separately ported; if a product string ever references them, the reference gate forces a doc. |
| `development.md`, `containerization.md`, `terminal-setup.md`, `termux.md`, `tmux.md`, `windows.md`, `shell-aliases.md`, `llama-cpp.md` | pi-platform or contributor guides; not applicable to cadr's SBCL build or are covered by README development sections. |

## 3a. Declared reason classes (goal 20 Part F)

A reason text whose normalized form (backtick contents and digits
stripped) is shared by two or more rows must belong to a class declared
here. The class owns the shared sentence shape; every member row still
carries its own row-specific citations inside the backticks, and members
are tagged `[class <id>]` at the end of the reason. Ratios are stated
against the 670 reason rows (development-policy section 5.4).

## 5. Audit boundary

This axis detects missing source capabilities but cannot detect a function whose symbol
exists while its internal behavior diverges. The streaming-space corruption found earlier
is the canonical counterexample. The behavior, source, non-functional, and presentation
ledgers therefore remain independent, and real terminal/provider acceptance remains
mandatory.
